innilaiyil oli māṟṟam evvaḷavu teḷivākat teriyum! moḻikku uyirāṉa olit tūymaiyē māṟum pōtu kālantōṟum citaiyum veṟṟuṭampu māṟiṉāl eṉṉa? talaiyē māṟukaiyil toppi māṟṟam oru kēṭākātu. “vēṟṟu vikāra viṭak kuṭampiṉ uṭkiṭappa āṟṟēṉ” eṉṟa tiruvācakappaṭi ayaloliyait tamiḻ vaṭivil eḻuti īraḻakaiyum keṭukka vēṇṭām.

tamiḻukkuttāṉā?
putiya oliyaic cērppataṉāl, paṇpaṭṭa oru moḻikkut tīya paṇpu pala pukuntu viṭum. putiya collākkam kuṟaiyum, taṭaippaṭum; paḻaiya coṟkaḷ tiripupaṭum; coṟpārvai kaṇṇuṟuttum. emmoḻiyum ulaka oliyaṉaittum uṭaiyatāya iruppatillai. uṭaiyatāka ākkiṉ ammoḻi kaṇkāṭci moḻiyāka viḷaṅkumēyaṉṟi vāymoḻiyāka vaḻaṅkātu. ulakameṅkum paraviya āṅkila moḻiyai nammaḷavil nōkkiṉum ivvuṇmai periyaveḻuttu ariccuvaṭi pōl viḷaṅkum. ḻa ḷa ṇa ṟa eṉṟa tamiḻ meyyolikaḷ āṅkilattil illai. aḻakappaṉ, vaḷḷuvar, aṇṇāmalai, maṟaimalai yaṭikaḷ, cōḻaṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ, tirukkuṟaḷ, cēkkiḻār, paḻani, āṭutuṟai, piḷḷai, viḻuppuram, vaṇṭalūr, paṇṇuruṭṭi, koḷḷiṭam,, cīrkāḻi, kuṟṟālam, tiruveṟumpūr mutaliya āṭpeyar, ūrppeyarc coṟkaḷai nam nāṭṭu āṅkila itaḻkaḷē eppaṭi eḻutukiṉṟaṉa? oli kalantō eḻuttuk kalantō acciṭukiṉṟaṉavā? piṉvarumāṟu eḻutip pārttāl aḻaku pulappaṭum.
ē) 	aḻakappaṉ 	... 	akappan
	vaḷḷuvar	 	... 	Vaḷḷuvar
	maṟaimalai 	... 	Maṟaimalai
	tirukkuṟaḷ 	... 	Tirukkuṟaḷ
	pāṇṭiyaṉ 	... 	Paṇtiyan
aṇṇāmalai 	... 	Aṇṇamalai

ivvāṟu nam āṅkilattāḷkaḷum āṅkila eḻuttāḷarkaḷum tamiḻukkē ciṟappoliyuṭaiya coṟkaḷai oli kalantum vaṭivu kalantum eḻutiṉāl akkāṭci kaṇṇaiyum karuttaiyum iṉpuṟuttumā? tuṉpuṟuttumā?
moḻi nērmai
tamiḻttāy eṉṉa kēṭkiṉṟāḷ? tamiḻ makaṉ ayal moḻikkuk kāṭṭum moḻi nērmaiyait taṉakkum kāṭṭumāṟu maṉṟāṭukiṉṟāḷ; vaḷarum ayalmoḻiyil kākkattuṭikkum moḻiyamaippai, vaḷarnta taṉmoḻiyilum kāttuk koḷḷumāṟu paṇikkiṉṟāḷ. olip perukkam vaḷarcci yākātu. olipalkiya vaṭamoḻi mutalāṉa intiya moḻikaḷ evvaḷavō uḷavē; vaḷarntuḷḷaṉavā? vaḷarccikku nūṟperukkamum kalvip perukkamum cintaṉaip perukkamum ivaṟṟaiyellām naṉku matikkum makkaṭ perukkamum aracuc cārpumē tēvai.
tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭa varalāṟṟaik kūrntu aṟinta varkaḷ ayaloliyai evvaḷavu vaṟpuṟuttit tiṇippiṉum tamiḻ ēṟṟuk koḷvatupōl kāṭṭit taḷḷiviṭuvatai aṟivar. vāyuṭ pukuntu oṭṭiya oru ciṟu vēṟṟup poruḷaiyum nā vaḷaittut tuḷaittu urāyntu eppaṭiyum veḷi yēṟṟuvatupōla, tamiḻum cilar ceyyuṭkaḷil cilar uṭainaṭaikaḷil avvappōtu pukunta vēṟṟolikaḷaik kālap pōkkil otukki viṭṭuviṭak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ivvuṇmaiyai aṟinta pavaṇantiyār vaṭacoṟkaḷ pala vāykkāl vaḻivanta iṭaikkālattu anta vaṭavolikaḷ iṉṉiṉṉavāṟu tamiḻmai peṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu virivilakkaṇam ceytār. vīramāmuṉivar tamiḻt tōṟṟam peṟṟu vāḻntatupōla, vaṭacoṟkaḷ tamiḻukku varumpōtu tammuruvattai akaṟṟik koṇṭu muḻut tamiḻuruvam peṟṟaṉa. ataṉāl accoṟkaḷ ōrāṟṟāl nilaipēṟu eytiṉa. vaṭavilakkaṇaṅkaḷaip peritum taḻuviya vīracōḻiyap puttamittiraṉārum tamiḻiṉ oliyamaippukku ūṟu cey tārillai. nāyaṉmārkaḷ, āḻvārkaḷ, kampar, villi puttūrār, parañcōtiyār eṉṟa perumakkaḷ ellām tamiḻmai kuṉṟāta pāṭalkaḷ āyiramāyiram eḻutiṉar.
vēṇṭum collākkaṅkaḷ paṭaitta piṉṉum, peyar vakaiyālō piṟavakaiyālō vēṟṟuc coṟkaḷai ōraḷavu taḻuvik koḷḷalām. itu vaḻivaḻi vanta tamiḻ marapē. aṅṅaṉam varum cila kiḷavikaḷ tam ayaṉmaiyai viṭṭut tamiḻmai peṟṟu oṉṟiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatum oru peruntamiḻ marapu. oṭṭāc coṟkaḷum oṭṭāccoṟkaḷāl āya kaṭṭuraikaḷum ceyyuḷkaḷum nūlkaḷum tamiḻiyaṟkaikkup puṟampāṉavai ātaliṉ avai oru kuṟippiṭṭa kālattaḷavukūṭa nilai nillā eṉpataṉai nalla eḻuttāḷap perumakkaḷ maṉaṅ koḷa vēṇṭum. viraintu varum putiya āytap pōkkai niṉaippiṉ ataṟku neṭum piṭippu illai. ciṉṉāḷil pal piṇiyuṟṟu maṟaintuviṭap pōkiṉṟatu.
vantavāṟu eṅṅaṉē pōmāṟu ētō
māyamām itaṟkētum makiḻa vēṇṭām
cintaiyīr umakkoṉṟu collak kēṇmiṉ
eṉpatu nāvukkaracar potuvākku. ātaliṉ eḻuttāḷarkaḷum ceytittāḷāḷarkaḷum taṅkay putiya karuttukkaḷait tamiḻiṉ nilaiyāṉa olikaḷait taḻuviyum eḻutuvārkaḷāka. putiya āytap pōkkaik kaiviṭuvārkaḷāka. vēṇṭumēl, pirāṉcu, pāram. puḷu. pilim, piliyōcā eṉṟu vanta ciṉ marapuppaṭiyāvatu eḻutuvārkaḷ eṉavum moḻi nērmaiyait tamiḻaṉṉaikkum pōṟṟit taruvarkaḷ eṉavum etir nōkkukiṉṟēṉ.


10. māṟṟoliyaṉ
tolkāppiyar tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ eḻuttu col poruḷ eṉṟa muttiṟattiṉaiyum ārāynta paḻam pērilakkaṇar. avarai ‘mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭi’ yavar eṉṟu pāyiram vitantu pukaḻkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl eḻuttiyal oliyiyal eṉṉum tuṟaiyil avarkku irunta taṉivaṉmai aṟiyappaṭum. ivvaṉmaikku eḻutattatikāramē kariyākum. cevippulaṉākiya olivaṭivu, kaṭpulaṉākiya varivaṭivu eṉa moḻikku iru vaṭivu uḷa. tolkāppiyar olivaṭivaiyē mutaṉmaiyākak koṇṭu ilakkaṇam eḻutiyavar. ‘ellā eḻuttum colluṅ kālaip piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala’ (83) eṉṉum nūṟpā yāppil ‘colluṅkālai’ eṉpataṉ viṉaip pakutiyai niṉaika. nākarika vaḷarcci karutumiṭattu, moḻiyiṉ varinilaiyum vilakka muṭiyātākaliṉ varivaṭivu paṟṟiyum tolkāppiyar ōraḷavu moḻintaṉar.
olittuṟai
vāyoliyiyal (ñāṭinēvaiuḷa) moḻiyoliyiyal (ñāṭinēaiuḷa) eṉa olittuṟai irupāṟpaṭum. kaṇ maruttuvarkkum pal maruttuvarkkum kaṇ pal uṟuppukkaḷiṉ taṉikkūṟum taṉi maruttuvamum naṉku teriyum. orutuṟai pōkiya aṉṉavar uṭaliṉ potukkūṟukaḷaiyum potu maruttuvattaiyum kaṭṭāyam aṟintirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. potunilai aṟintārkkut tāṉē ciṟapput tuṟai naṉiviḷaṅkum. tolkāppiyar moḻiyoliñar; tamiḻ eṉṉum oru moḻiyiṉ olikaḷai ārāynta nuṇṇiyar; ‘eḻutteṉappaṭupa akaramutal ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵteṉpa’ eṉṟa mutaṟ pāvilēyē tamiḻiṉ oliyaṉkaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi nūl toṭaṅkiyavar. oru moḻittuṟai muṭṭaṟutta ivar vāyoliñarum āvār. ‘uruviṉum icaiyiṉum arukit tōṉṟum moḻik kuṟippellām eḻuttiṉ iyalā’  (40), ‘unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi’  (83), ‘ellā eḻuttum veḷippaṭakkiḷantu’ (102) eṉṟiṉṉa nūṟpākkaḷāl vāyolikaḷiṉ cila kūṟukaḷait tolkāppiyar kāṭṭic celvar. ittakaiya irupēroliñar iyaṟṟiya eḻuttatikāram muṟṟum oliyatikāramātaliṉ, iṟṟaiya oliñarkaḷiṉ kūriya pārvaikku uriyatu. pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarntu vaḻakkoṭuvarum tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ ceṟinta olippaṇpukaḷait tolkāppiya vāyilākak kāṇpatum, kaṇṭu olittuṟai yārāyccikku utavuvatum nam kaṭaṉ.
oliyaṉ
oliyaṉ (Phoneme) eṉappaṭuvatu yātu? vēṟu teḷinta oli mutal nilai eṉpar puḷūmpīlṭu. uṟavuṭaiyaṉavum, colliṭait tamakkeṉat taṉi niṟpiṭaṉ uṭaiyaṉavum ākiya oru moḻiyiṉ olik kuṭumpam eṉpar tāṉiyal cōṉcu. okkum piṟappiṭamum ovvā varaviṭamum uṭaiya olikaḷiṉ iṉam eṉpar kiḷicaṉār.
“The confusion of usage with the term ‘Phoneme’ is unfortunately not unique in linguistic terminology. But in the case of the phoneme this confusion is particularly distressing because of the relatively recent introduction of the term” 
eṉṟu pirimeṉ tātal oliyaṉ ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya nīṇṭa āyvuraiyil kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. (on defining the Phoneme - Reading in liguistics). itaṉāl vāymoḻikku mutalāṉa oliyaṉiṉ taṉmai mēlum kāṇṭaṟkuriyatu eṉpatum, itukāṟum palar varainta oliyaṉ ilakkaṇattai vaittuk koṇṭu, tamiḻ olik kūṟukaḷait tuṇital kaṭai pōkātu eṉpatum pōtarum. oliyaṉ paṟṟit tolkāppiyattāl aṟiyalākum karuttuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟai viḷakkuvāṉ ikkaṭṭurai varaikiṉṟēṉ.
	     1.		    2.
	kaṭam			māṉ
	caṭam			māṇ
	taṭam			māl
	paṭam			māḷ
	naṭam			māḻ
	maṭam			māy
mutaṟpakutiyil (k c t p n m) mutal meyyoliyiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum, iraṇṭān tokutiyil (ṉ ṇ l ḷ ḻ y) īṟṟu meyyoliyiṉ vēṟupāṭṭālum coltōṟum poruḷ vēṟuvarak kāṇkiṉṟōm. .iv vēṟṟumaikku olivēṟṟumaiyē - avvoli vevvēṟāṉa paḷḷiyil (point of articulation) piṟappatuvē kāraṇamām. ātaliṉ poruḷ vēṟṟumai ceyyum piṟappu vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiya olikaḷai orumoḻiyiṉ oliyaṉkaḷ (Phonemes) eṉṟu terikiṉṟōm. poruḷaṭippaṭaiyil oliyārāycci nikaḻtal kūṭātu eṉpatu oliñar pallōr kōṭpāṭāyiṉum akkōḷ ōrellai yaḷavē cellum. vāyoliyāyvukkē porunti varum. oru moḻippaṭuttum oliyai āyuṅkālai, poruṭpārvai nilaikkaḷam ākum. ‘meyteri vaḷiyicai’ (102) eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. mey-poruḷ. oru moḻiyiṉ oliyaṉ piṟitoru moḻiyil māṟṟoliyāka (allophone) irukkalām; oru moḻiyiṉ māṟṟoli maṟṟoru moḻiyil oliyaṉāka uyarntu viḷaṅkalām. ivvellām poruḷ paṟṟittāṉē aṟutiyiṭappaṭum.
eḻuttup pōli
nūṉmarapu moḻimarapu piṟappiyal eṉṉum mūṉṟiyalkaḷ eḻuttatikārattu mutaṟkaṇ uḷavē. ivai tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ olittiṟaṅkaḷai aṟivippaṉa. nūṉmarapu tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷaic cuṭṭi viḷakkukiṉṟatu. eḻuttu eṉṉum colliṟku oliyaṉ eṉpatuvē mutaṟporuḷ. moḻi marapil ivvoliyaṉkaḷ oru colliṭaip paṭuṅkāl paṭum taṉmaikaḷai viḷakkuvar āciriyar. piṟappiyalil ivvoliyaṉkaḷ piṟakkum paḷḷi kaḷaiyum muyaṟcikaḷaiyum taṉit taṉiyākap pulappaṭuttuvar. piṉṉar varum puṇariyalkaḷil tamiḻoliyaṉkaḷ coṟkaḷiṉ toṭariṭaip paṭuṅkāl uṟum olittiripukaḷai virivāka aṟikiṉṟōm. iṉi moḻi marapiyalil colliya oliyaṉpaṟṟiya karuttoṉṟaik kāṇpām.
1. 	aivaṉam 		- 	aivaṉam
2. 	aivaṉam 		- 	ayvaṉam
3. 	oḷavai 		- 	auvai
4. 	nāi 		- 	nāy
5. 	akaṉ	 	- 	akam
mēlaik kāṭṭukkaḷaip pōli allatu eḻuttuppōli eṉpar tamiḻnūlōr. ikkuṟiyīṭu tolkāppiyattu illai. itaṉai mutaṟkaṇ uraikkiṭai āḷupavar iḷampūraṇarē. ‘mutalīṟiṭai nilai pōli’ eṉṟu naṉṉūlār oli yiyalpukaḷai vakuttuk koḷvar. ip pōliyai uyirppōli, meyppōli, piṟapōli eṉṟu mūṉṟu vakaiyākak koḷḷalām. aivaṉam - aivaṉam eṉpatu uyirppōli. akaṉ - akam eṉpatu meyppōli. aivaṉam - ayavaṉam; nāi - nāy eṉpaṉa piṟa pōli. oliyaṉ āvatu poruḷum piṟappum terintu teḷinta olimutaṟkūṟu. māṟṟoliyāvatu poruḷum piṟappum ottu, varavu (distribution) vēṟu uṭaiya puṭaiyoli. pōliyāvatu poruḷum varavum ottup piṟappu vēṟu uṭaiya māṟṟoliyaṉ; aḵtāvatu ōr oliyaṉ niṟkumiṭattu vēṟu ōr oliyaṉ varum. itu poruḷ māṟṟañ ceyyātu oliyaṉai maṭṭum māṟṟi niṟṟaliṉ māṟṟoliyaṉ (allophoneme) eṉṟu peyar peṟum.
tamiḻ oliyaṉkaḷuḷ iṅṅaṉam oru collil vantu māṟṟi niṟkavalla oliyaṉkaḷait tolkāppiyar moḻi marapil eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar.
akara ikaram aikāram ākum   	(21)
akara ukaram oḷakāram ākum   	(22)
eṉṟa nūṟpākkaḷāl, ‘ai’ eṉṉum ōroliyaṉukku ‘ai’ eṉṉum iraṇṭoliyaṉkaḷum ‘oḷa’ eṉṉum ōroli yaṉukku ‘au’ eṉṉum iraṇṭoliyaṉkaḷum iṇaintu vantu māṟṟoliyaṉ ākum. ‘ākum’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyar viṉaimuṭipu ceytiruttaliṉ iraṇṭoliyaṉ kūṭi ōroliyaṉakku īṭākutal ceyaṟkai eṉpatu karuttām.
akarat timpar yakarap puḷḷiyum
aiyeṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum
iraṇṭu uyiroliyaṉkaḷ ōr uyiroliyaṉukku māṟṟāka īṭāka varuvatai mēlē kaṇṭōm. innūṟ pāvāl uyirum meyyum (ay) iṇaintu, aikārattiṉ māṟṟoli yaṉāka varuvatait taṉittu eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvar. ‘ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum’ (58) eṉpataṉāl uyiroliyaṉukku meyyoliyaṉ māṟṟāka varuvatu kāṇka. ituvarai viḷakkiya pōlikaḷ (ai, au, ay, y) ellām tattamakku uriya uyiroliyaṉkaḷiṉ (ai, oḷa, i) icaiyōṭu otticaippavai; cevippulaṉukku ōraṉṉa ōcai tarupavai. ‘ai yottu icaikkum’ ‘oḷa vōraṉṉa’ eṉṟu pavaṇantiyār pōliyiṉ icai yoppumaiyai veḷippaṭuttuvar. ‘cila eḻuttukkaḷ kūṭic cila eḻuttukkaḷ pōla icaikkum eṉa eḻuttup pōli kūṟukiṉṟatu’ eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵteṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa   	(82)
colliṭai oru meyyoliyaṉukku vēṟoru meyyoliyaṉ māṟṟoliyaṉāka varum eṉpatu ippōli nūṟpāviṉ karuttu (puṟaṉ - puṟam). ōr uyiroliyaṉukku iraṇṭu oliyaṉkaḷ māṟṟāka vanta nilai meyyoliyaṉukku illai. uyirkku vanta māṟṟoliyaṉ avvuyirōcaiyōṭu ottu niṟpatupōla meykku vanta māṟṟoliyaṉ ammeyyōcai yaik kāṭṭi niṟpatumillai. pōliyuḷḷum ivvēṟupāṭu kuṟikkattakum.
pōli varampu
moḻikku oliyaṉkaḷ aṭippaṭaiyāṉavai. ivaṟṟai mutaleḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu muntu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟam.  ōroliyaṉukku māṟṟoliyaṉ varumeṉiṉ, oliyaṉiṉ taṉittaṉmaiyum ciṟappum kuṉṟāvā? colnilaiyil ikarattukku yakaravoṟṟum ṉakarattukku makaramum varumāyiṉ, ivaṟṟai vēṟu vēṟu oliyaṉkaḷāka eṇṇuvatu piḻaiyaṉṟō? ivai nalviṉākkaḷē. moḻiyil varum tiripukaḷukkellām oru peru varampu uṇṭu; atu poruḷ varampākum. ōroli kuṉṟal keṭal māṟal eṉat tiripupala uṟalām. ettiripu nilaiyilum, taṉakku uriya poruḷait tiriyātu taṉ moḻik kulattārkku (Speech community) teḷiyak kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. kāṭṭumāyiṉ, avvolittiripai ak kulattār poruṭpaṭuttuvatillai. aiyar - ayyar; vairam - vayiram; oḷavai - avvai; aracu - araicu eṉavarum oliyaṉ māṟṟattāl muṉṉaip poruḷ vēṟupaṭāmai kāṇka. ‘akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum........ meypeṟat tōṉṟum’ (56) eṉṟu atē poruḷ tarutalait tolkāppiyar kuṟippar. ōroliyaṉukku varum māṟṟoliyaṉ ellāviṭattum vārā. varumāyiṉaṉṟō attakaiya iraṇṭoliyaṉ iruppatu vīṇākum.
avaṉ 	- 	avam
araṉ 	- 	aram
nīlaṉ		- 	nīlam
kālaṉ 	- 	kālam
āyaṉ 	- 	āyam
kōlaṉ 	- 	kōlam
pālaṉ 	- 	pālam
iccoṟkaḷ nalla poruḷ vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyavai. ātaliṉ ṉakaravoliyaṉum makaravoliyaṉum tam taṉittaṉmaiyōṭu vantaṉavākavē iccoṟkaḷiṟ koḷḷa vēṇṭum. eṉavē poruḷ māṟum eṉiṉ, pōli eṉṟa māṟṟoliyaṉukku iṭamillai eṉpatu tuṇipu.
iruvakai varicai
oliyaṉ māṟṟattukkum oru varampu uṇṭu. atu poruḷ varampākum eṉṟu mēlē cuṭṭiṉēṉ. poruḷukku yātoru teḷivukkuṟaivum ēṟpaṭātavāṟu, ēṟpaṭāta nilaivarai oliyaṉai māṟṟic collalāmā? māṟṟoliyaṉukku iṭaṅkoṭāc col varicaiyum iṭaṅkoṭukkum colvaricaiyum eṉa iruvakai tamiḻ moḻikkaṇṇē kāṇappaṭum.
	vilai 			vāl
	viḷai 			vāḷ
	viḻai 			vāḻ
ivai iṭaṅkoṭāc colvaricai., iccollamaip pil mūṉṟu oliyaṉkaḷum (l ḷ ḻ) vanta coṟkaḷ iruttaliṉ, māṟṟoliyaṉ varavukku vāyppillai. kaṇ kaṉ, maṇ maṉ, māṇ māṉ eṉpaṉavum aṉṉa
	pukal 		kāl 		pāl
	pukaḻ 		kāḻ 			pāḻ
ivai iṭaṅ koṭukkum colvaricai. iccolla maippil ḷakara oliyaṉ koṇṭa coṟkaḷ tamiḻil illai. ātaliṉ ḷakaram māṟṟoliyaṉākap pukutaṟkum pukaḷ, kāḷ, pāḷ eṉṟa coṟpōlikaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkum iṭaṉuṇṭu. ataṉālaṉṟō pavaḻam - pavaḷam; uḻuntu - uḷuntu; kaḻuttu - kaḷuttu eṉa māṟṟoliyaṉkaḷ vanta col vaḻakkāṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu. immāṟṟattāl poruḷ ūṟupaṭāmaiyum kāṇka. ūṟupaṭata takka col muṉṉarē moḻikkaṇ iṉmaiyum kāṇka. ḷakara ḻakara oliyaṉkaḷai makkaḷ poruṭpaṭutti oliyāmaikku ituvē kāraṇamām.
maṅkalam : matil
ivaiyum māṟṟoliyaṉukku vāyppaḷikkum coṟkaḷ, iccollamaippil ḷakara ḻakara oliyaṉkaḷuṭaiya kiḷavikaḷ illai. ātaliṉ eppaṭi iccoṟkaḷai olippiṉum moḻik kulattuc cevikaḷ vāṅkik koṇṭaṉa. maṅkaḷam, matiḷ eṉṟa col vaṭivaṅkaḷai ilakkiyattum kāṇkiṉṟōm. uḻuntu uḷuntu, maṅkalam maṅkaḷam eṉṟa kāṭṭukkaḷāl ivvaricaiyil māṟṟoliyaṉāka varuvatu ḷakaramātal aṟiyattakum. muṉtōṉṟum lakarattukkum piṉtōṉṟum ḻakarattukkum iṭait tōṟṟamuṭaiyatu ḷakaramēyākum.  ātaliṉ uluntu eṉavum maṅkaḻam eṉavum māṟṟoliyaṉ peṟāmaikkuk kāraṇam peṟappaṭum. kailai-kayilai, muracu-muraicu; putal-putar; ñāyiṟu-nāyiṟu; ñēyam-nēyam eṉṟa pōlikaḷ ellām mēṟkāṭṭiyapaṭi iṭaṅ koṭukkum coṟkaḷil vantavaiyē.
maṅkalam, maṅkaḷam; uḻuntu, uḷuntu; kuṭar, kuṭal eṉpaṉavaṟṟai oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi eṉak koḷḷalāmā? malai, varai, ōṅkal, aṭukkam eṉpaṉa oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. iccoṟkaḷil oliyaṉkaḷ mika vēṟupaṭṭuḷa. maṅkalam, maṅkaḷam eṉpavaṟṟil pakka oliyaṉkaḷ ellām ottiruppa. ōriṭattuttāṉ oliyaṉ māṟṟam uṇṭu.; am māṟṟamum piṟappil aṇimaiyuṭaiyatu. ātaliṉ māṟṟoliyaṉ koṇṭa pōlic coṟkaḷ oru poruṭ paṉmoḻiyākā. oliyaṉkaḷ toṭarum oru collil - iṭaṅkoṭukkum oru colliṭai - māṟṟoliyaṉ varumātaliṉ, tolkāppiyar orumoḻittaṉmai kūṟum moḻi marapil pōli kuṟittu nūṟpākkaḷ yāttār. māṟṟoliyaṉ varavu pōliyēyātaliṉ, maṅkalam, aracu, muracu, mutalāṉa taṉṉoliyaṉuṭaiya coṟkaḷ eṉṟum oru moḻiyil vāḻum; vīḻā eṉa aṟika. mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟai kāṭṭiyavar tolkāppiyar eṉṟa  pārāṭṭiṉ meymmaiyai aṟintu tolkāppiyattai muṉvaittu nam moḻiyārāycciyait toṭaṅkuvōmāka.


11. pāvam
tamiḻmoḻi cila pattāṇṭukaḷākap pala tuṟaiyilum taḻaittup perukukiṉṟatu. urimai yuṇarcciyē ataṟkuk kāraṇam. viṭutalaiyuṇarvō namakku moḻi yuṇarvōṭu tōṉṟiyatu. nam tāymoḻi tāḻātu vaḷara vēṇṭum; aracāḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa viḻaivaiyum viṭutalaikku oru peruṅ kāraṇamāyp paṟaicāṟṟiṉōm. pulavar perumakaṉ pāratiyār nāṭu moḻi eṉṉum iru perum paṟṟaiyum oruṅku kuḻaittu makkaṭku ūṭṭiṉār. mītūrnta moḻiyaṉpē āntirap piriviṉaikku ētu eṉpatu veḷippaṭai.
ceytittāḷkaḷ vaḻakkut tamiḻiṉ vaḷarccikku naṟṟuṇai ceykiṉṟaṉa. paṭakkāṭcikaḷ moḻiyaip poṟuttavarai, tamiḻākkattaip peṇkaḷiṭattum kuḻantaikaḷ iṭattum parappukiṉṟaṉa. itu kuṭiyaracuk kālam. makkaṭkuk karuttuk koḷuttavum, aṉṉavarait tañcārpāka iḻukkavum kāṭciyāḷarkaḷ paṟṟum potuk karuvi tamiḻ aṉṟō? immuṟaiyāl tamiḻuyir nīṭittu viṭṭatu; ataṉ āṭci kālkoṇṭu viṭṭatu. uyarnilaip paḷḷivarai tamiḻmoḻi kalvimoḻiyāy amainta nāḷ toṭṭu, ataṉ celvākku “tamiḻil muṭiyumā?” eṉṟu iṭaiyūṟāyk kiṭanta oru cārārai añci viyakkumpaṭi ceytatu. tamiḻakak kallūrikaḷum palkalaik kaḻakaṅkaḷum nilattut tamiḻiṉaik karuttu vāyilākak koḷḷum kālam oṉṟu varum; akkālam aṇittu varuvatu aṟivuk kaṇṇukku atō tōṉṟukiṟatu. aññāṉṟu tamiḻ - uṭaṉ vāḻnta ulakat toṉmoḻi yellām vaḻakku oḻiyavum oḻiyā uyirt tamiḻ - ñāla araṅkil naṭam puriyak kāṇpōm.
moḻiyaṭippaṭaiyil intiya nāṭu pakutippaṭṭu viṭṭatu. iṉi avvan nilattumoḻi parantōṅkic cemmākkum eṉpatu uṟuti. mēlum, tamiḻ vaḻaṅku nilam tamiḻakam maṭṭumaṉṟu. tilli, pampāy, pūṉā, kalkattā mutaliya intiyap piṟa nilaṅkaḷ tamiḻttāy kuṭikoṇṭirukkum putu māḷikaikaḷ. malēyā tamiḻp paiṅkūḻukku oru putuvayal. ilaṅkai tamiḻaracuk kumariyiṉ araṇmaṉai. teṉṉāppirikkā tamiḻ naṅkaiyiṉ uṟaviṭam. iv vaṇṇam paḻantamiḻ iṭattāṟ parantu ceyalāl nilaittu vāḻvataik kāṇutoṟum kāṇutoṟum tamiḻ neñcaṅkaḷ vimmip pūrikkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟcolliyāṅku nam tāymoḻi palapaṭap paraviṉālum ataṉ vaḷarcci aṟiñar palaraik kavaravillai. it tamiḻpperukkam nilaiyā? mēṟpōkkā? vēr ūṉṟumā? vaḻakkōṭu iraṇṭaṟak kalakkumā? vaḷarvatu pōlak kāṭṭi, paḻaiya perukkattaiyum vārik koṇṭu pōyviṭumā? moḻiyaṭippaṭaiyaiyē takarttuc citaittu urumāṟṟiviṭumā? ivvaḷarvukkut tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar poruntumā? eṉa ivvāṟu pala uḷḷaṅkaḷ ucāvi alaikiṉṟaṉa. inta aiyaṅkaḷ mayakkattālō, poṟāmaiyālō, cūḻcciyālō eḻuntaṉa valla. aṟivāl eḻunta aiyaṅkaḷ. ivaṟṟukkut talaiyāya oru kāraṇattai māttiram ikkaṭṭuraiyiṟ kāṇpōm.
ilakkiyac cuvaiyaip parappuvāruḷ oru cilar ‘pāvam’eṉṟa collaic cila kālamākap peritum kaiyāḷupa. uḷḷak kuṟippu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. tamiḻil muṉpē pāvam eṉap piṟitoru col uṇṭu. tīviṉai eṉpatu ataṟkup poruḷ. putiya kuṟippup pāvattaip paḻaiya tīviṉaip pāvattiṉum vēṟu paṭuttuvataṟkup pāvattiṉ mutaleḻuttait taṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟārkaḷ. ipparuveḻuttu moḻi muṟaiyaṉṟu; valintu pukuttiṉālum kaivaḻakkil oṭṭuvataṉṟu; tamiḻ varampu aṉṟu; ataṟku vaḷarcciyum aṉṟu. ōreḻuttaiyē paṭṭaiyākavum taṭṭaiyākavum parumaṉākavum ciṟumaṉākavum cāyvākavum nērākavum kaṇṭavāṟu yār kaiyum eḻutak kāṇkiṉṟōm. oḻuṅkillā innilaiyil, parumai ciṟumai eṉṟiṉṉa oreḻuttiṉ tōṟṟa vēṟupāṭukaṭ kellām poruḷ vēṟu vēṟu kūṟap pukiṉ, emmoḻiyum viḷaṅkātu viraivil iṭukāṭu aṭaiyum.
ōr olikku ōreḻuttu eṉpatu moḻiyaṟam. oli vēṟu poruḷ vēṟu koṇṭāl eḻuttum vēṟu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. muṟai māṟi īrolikku ōreḻuttuk koḷvatum, avveḻuttaiyē paṭṭai pōṭuvatum aṟappiḻai; moḻic citaivu. inti vaṭamoḻikaḷum iṉavolikaḷukku vevvēṟu taṉiyeḻuttuk koṇṭaṉavē yaṉṟi, ōreḻuttuḷḷēyē viḷampum vēlai ceyyavillai. eṇṇiṟanta vaṭamoḻik kiḷavikaḷ iṭaikkālat tamiḻil pukuntaṉa. avvellām tam moḻimai yiḻantupukkakattu maṇamakaḷ pōlat tamiḻmai eytaṉa; tam oliyai viṭṭut tamiḻoli koṇṭu viḷaṅkiṉa.* ivviṉiya moḻiyākkamē nilaippatu; piṉpaṟṟat takuvatu. tamiḻukku vantum kalavātu taṉittu niṟkum piṟa coṟkaḷ kālappōkkil tāmē akalum; allatu aṟivup puraṭciyāl akaṟṟappaṭum.
kuṟippu eṉṉum tamiḻc col muṉpē namakku uṇṭu. ataṉaip pērāṭcikkuk koṇṭu varuvatu kaṟṟār kaṭaṉ, uriya col tamiḻil iruppavum, ‘pāvam’ eṉṟa piṟa moḻic col vēṇṭuvatillai. orukāl āḷavirumpiṉ, tamiḻp ‘pāvam’ āka oliyāmal vaṭamoḻip pāvam ākavē vaittu olippatu poruntātu. vaḻakkil yārum eḻuti varum iyalpup pakara vaṭivāka eḻutāmal, parutta pakaramāka acciṭal evvāṟṟāṉum ovvātu. kaṭaṉ paṭṭālum varampu pōṟṟit tamiḻ vaḷarkkum vāḻvu namakku vēṇṭum. tolkāppiyar mutalāya cāṉṟōr tamiḻukku oliyilakkaṇam vakuttaṉarēyaṉṟi, eḻutuvār kaikku eṟpa ettuṇaiyō vēṟupaṭum variyilakkaṇam varaintār allar. tamiḻukku aṭippaṭaiyai oli nilaiyil kaṭṭiṉarē yaṉṟi varivaṭiviṟ kaṭṭiṉār allar. tamiḻ varampu ituvātaliṉ, ōreḻuttut taṉṉaiyē taṭippittup poruḷ kāṭṭalum, varivaṭivait tamiḻ moḻikku uyirākkalum aṭimuraṇ eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
ipputup pāvac col paṟṟip piṟitoṉṟum niṉaikka vēṇṭum. oru col nallatu tīyatu eṉa iru poruṭkum uriyatāyiṉ, kālappōkkil tīyatu nallatai aḻikkum. ittīyaveṟṟi moḻiyaṟam; tamiḻ kāṭṭum varalāṟu. irutaṉmaikkum uriyaoru collai uraikkum pōtu, kēṭpārkkut tīyaporuḷē muṉṉiṟkum ātaliṉ, nalla poruḷiṟ colla yārum tuṇiyār. talaivaṉ poruḷīṭṭak kaḷḷik kāṭṭiṉaik kaṭantu ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟa karuttaik ‘kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭu iṟantōr’ (kuṟun-16) eṉpāḷ caṅkat talaivi. ‘puli cettu verīiya pukarmuka vēḻam’ (akam-12) eṉṟa aṭikkaṇ, puli cettu-puli yeṉṟu karuti eṉpatu poruḷ. ‘māṇṭa eṉ maṉaiviyoṭu makkaḷum nirampiṉar’ (puṟam. 191) eṉṟa toṭaril māṇṭa - ciṟanta eṉpatu poruḷ. ivvāṟu caṅkat tamiḻar naṟporuḷil vaḻaṅkiya iṟanta, cetta, māṇṭa eṉṟa coṟkaḷukku varavarac cāvup poruḷkaḷ ēṟiviṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl, avaṟṟai nalla poruḷavāy makkaḷ āḷat tuṇiyavillai. avaṟṟiṉ naṟkūṟu vaḻakkoḻintatu. itu tamiḻ moḻi varalāṟu. ivviḷakkattāl nām aṟiyak kiṭappatu eṉṉa? pāvam eṉṟa putuc collai nalla kuṟippup poruḷāy iṉṟu nām āṇṭālum, paḻaiya tīviṉaip poruḷē kēṭpārkku muṉ tōṉṟum; kuṟippup poruḷ tāṉē oḻintupōm. ātalāl ipputiya pāvattai - parutta pāva eḻuttai - tamiḻ eḻuttāḷar collāḷar piṉpaṟṟa vēṇṭā eṉṟu vēṇṭikkoḷvaṉ. ikaliṉṟi ivvuṇmaiyai ārāyntu koḷka.


12. ātticūṭiyum eḻuttiyalum
oḷavaiyār pāṭiya ātticūṭi ētti ētti ārāytaṟku uriyatu. kāraṇam innūl ciṟitāyiṉum putumaiyum oḻuṅkum uṭaiyatu. ātticūṭikku uraikaḷum kataikaḷum palavuḷa; ilakkiya viḷakkaṅkaḷum camaya viḷakkaṅkaḷum pala tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa. pāratiyār ātticūṭi, pāratitācaṉ ātticūṭi eṉa vaḻi nūlkaḷum eḻuntuḷa. āṅkila moḻi peyarppukkaḷum uṇṭu. piṟamoḻikaḷilum iccūṭi moḻi peyarkkappaṭum eṉṟu etirpārkkalām. nīti nūlkaḷil tirukkuṟaḷukku aṭutta ciṟappai ātticūṭi peṟṟu varukiṉṟatu. vaḷḷuvarpōl oḷavaiyum nāṭum vīṭum aṟiyap peṟṟavar.
ilakkaṇap pārvai
ātticūṭi palanilaiyil ārāyappaṭṭiruntālum ilakkaṇa nōkkil eḻunta ārāycci illai. 108 ātticūṭikaḷum evvakaippā ākum? cūttiram allatu nūṟpā eṉṟu peyariṭukiṉṟōm. ‘cūttiram yāppiṉuḷ tōṉṟa yāttamaippatuvē’ eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvataṉṟi evvakai yāppu eṉṟu cuṭṭavillai. ‘cilvakai yeḻuttiṟ palvakaip poruḷai’ eṉṟa nūṟpāvilum cūttirattiṉ taṉmai kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟic ceyyuḷ vakai kuṟikkappaṭavillai. āciriyappāvilum veṇpāvilum kaṭṭaḷaik kalit tuṟaiyilum ilakkaṇa nūṟpākkaḷ amaintiruppataik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ātticūṭiyuḷ nūṟu, aṟañceya virumpu, āṟuvatu ciṉam eṉa irucīrkaḷāl amaintavai. ēṉai eṭṭum ṅappōl vaḷai, neṟpayir viḷai eṉa oru cīr koṇṭu mēlum ōracaiyāl muṭipavai. āṉāl muḻutum orucīr ātticūṭiyō muccīr ātti cūṭiyō illai.
etukai mōṉai mutalāṉa toṭaivikaṟpam paṟṟiyum ārāyvataṟku uriyatu icciṟu nūl. toṭai eṉpataṉ potuvilakkaṇam eṉṉa? ōr oḻuṅkāṉa iyakkam. nāṟcīrkaḷilum ōr oḻuṅku iruppiṉ muṟṟākum. iṟutic cīrkaḷiṉ oḻuṅku iyaipām. mutaṟ cīrkaḷiṉ oḻuṅku mōṉaiyām. antam mutalāt toṭuppatu antāti eṉṟu kārikai oru pāṭṭiṟkuḷ antātit toṭai vakukkiṉṟatu. ivvilakkaṇattai virivupaṭuttit tiruvāymoḻi pōṉṟa toṭarnilaic ceyyuṭkaḷaiyum antāti nūl eṉak koḷkiṉṟōm. ātticūṭiyiṉ amaippilum oru moḻi yoḻuṅku uṇṭu. uyireḻuttukkaḷaiyum meyyeḻuttuk kaḷaiyum uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷaiyum mutalākak koṇṭu 108 ceyyuṭkaḷai niralpaṭak kūṟukiṉṟatē; yāppil itaṟkoru peyar vaikka vēṇṭāmā? ēṟkaṉavē uḷḷa peyar vakaiyil itu aṭaṅkavillai. oḷavai ātticūṭiyum koṉṟai vēntaṉum piṉ eḻunta ātticūṭikaḷum neṭuṅkaṇakkut toṭaiyuṭaiyavai eṉṟu oru pututtoṭai koḷḷalām. ātticūṭiyiṉ ceyyuḷ toṭaipaṟṟip piṉ eḻunta yāppilakkaṇaṅkaḷ karuttuc celuttavillai eṉpatu mēl eḻutiya cila kuṟippukkaḷāl pōtarum.
mutaleḻuttukkaḷ
ikkaṭṭurai ātticūṭi nūlil kāṇattakum moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷ kuṟittu ōr oppīṭṭārāycci ceyya muṟpaṭukiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyil kālantōṟum māṟum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uḷa; māṟāta ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum uḷa, māṟum ilakkaṇattiliruntu moḻivaralāṟu aṟiya muṭikiṉṟatu. eḻuttiyalil moḻikku mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭaiyil varum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉa mūṉṟu nilaikaḷ uḷa, tolkāppiyattilum naṉṉūlilum mutaleḻuttu iṟutiyeḻuttukkaḷ paṟṟi vēṟupāṭukaḷ uṇṭu. iṭainilai mayakkam paṟṟi yātum māṟṟam illai. itaṉāl kālattil māṟum ilakkaṇak kūṟu etu, māṟātatu etu eṉṟu terintu teḷiya muṭikiṉṟatu. mutaṉilaiyilum īṟṟu nilaiyilum paḻaiyaṉa kaḻittup putiyaṉa taḻuviya naṉṉūlār iṭainilai mayakkattil kālamāṟutalai ēṟṟuk koṇṭilar.
iṇaintiyal kālai yaralak kikaramum
mavvak kukaramum nakarak kakaramum
micaivarum ravvaḻi yuvvu māmpiṟa 	(naṉ. 149)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyattiṉ marapaik kāttu valiyuṟuttuvar.
tamiḻ moḻi varalāṟṟil mutaleḻuttup paṟṟiya māṟutalkaḷai ārāyappukum nām tolkāppiyam vīra cōḻiyam nēminātam  naṉṉūl ivaṟṟiṉ uraikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟai mēṟkōḷākak koḷkiṉṟōm. mutaleḻuttiṉ ārāycci ilakkaṇa nūlaḷavil curuṅki niṟkiṉṟatu. ivvārāyccikku mutaleḻuttukkaḷaiyē aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu pāṭiya ātticūṭiyum ataṉai yotta koṉṟaivēntaṉum uriya nūlkaḷ ām eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṟa īramaippum koṇṭa ātticūṭiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu amaippu piṉvarumāṟu. koṉṟaivēntaṉ amaippaiyum uṭaṉ kāṇalām.
ātticūṭiyiṉ ceyyuṭkaḷ 108.
koṉṟai vēntaṉ ceyyuṭkaḷ 91



					ātticūṭi		koṉṟai

kūṭutal		108	91
		-------	-------
1. paṉṉīruyirum mutaṉmoḻiyāka varum eṉṟa nilaiyil innūlukkum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukkum māṟupāṭillai.
2. oḷaviyam pēcēl, kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl eṉṟa iruceyyuṭkum iṭaiyil aḵkam curukkēl eṉṟa ceyyuḷ uḷḷatu. itaṉāl oḷavaiyār uyirukkum meykkum naṭuvē āytattaik koṇṭār eṉavum mutaleḻuttai muppattoṉṟāka eṇṇiṉār eṉavum karutalām.
aṟinta veḻuttammuṉ paṉṉiraṇ ṭāvikaḷāṉa kammuṉ
piṟanta patiṉeṭṭu mey; naṭuvāytam 
eṉṟa vīracōḻiyam oḷavaiyiṉ karuttukku ottiruttal kāṇka.
3. meyyeḻuttu mutaṉmoḻi paṟṟi ātticūṭiyil pala cikkalkaḷ uḷa. 14-31 varaiyuḷḷa patiṉeṭṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷ 18 meykaḷum moḻi mutalāka vantaṉa pōl eḻutappaṭṭavai. ivai nuṇukiya āyvukku uriya ātaliṉ, avaṟṟiṉai īṇṭu eṭutteḻutik koḷvatu nallatu.
1. 	kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl
2. 	ṅappōl vaḷai
3. 	caṉi nīrāṭu
4. 	ñayampaṭavurai
5. 	iṭampaṭa vīṭeṭēl
6. 	iṇakka maṟintiṇaṅku
7. 	tantai tāyppēṇ
8. 	naṉṟi maṟavēl
9. 	paruvattē payircey
10. 	maṉṟu paṟittuṇṇēl
11. 	iyalpalātaṉa ceyēl
12. 	aravamāṭṭēl
13. 	ilavam pañciṟṟuyil
14. 	vañcakam pēcēl
15. 	aḻakalātaṉa ceyēl
16. 	iḷamaiyiṟkal
17. 	aṟaṉai maṟavēl
18. 	aṉantalāṭēl
ippatiṉeṭṭum kakaramutal ṉakara iṟutiyākavum, iṉaveḻuttu aṭuttaṭuttu varavum amaintuḷa. neṭuṅkaṇakkuppaṭi ṟakaraṉakaram iṟutiyil uḷḷaṉa. itaṉāl patineṭṭu meyyiṉaiyum mutaṉilaiyāka oḷavaiyār koṇṭār eṉṟu colluvataiviṭa appatiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum neṭuṅ kaṇakkuppaṭi niralpaṭa vaittukkāṭṭa eṇṇiṉār eṉṟu koḷvatē poruttam. aṅṅaṉam kāṭṭiyavar moḻi mutalil vārā eṉṟu karutiya ṭa ṇa ya ra la ḻa ḷa ṟa ṉa eṉṟa oṉpatu meyyeḻuttukkaḷai oṟṟākavē vaittuk kāṭṭiyirukkalām. iṭampaṭa, iṇakkam, aravam eṉa uyirmeynilaiyil ēṉ kūṟiṉār eṉpatu teriyavillai. āṉāl patiṉeṭṭuc ceyyuṭkaḷilum uḷḷa mutaleḻuttukkaḷ ellām kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷām eṉpatum avaṟṟai uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttu, uyirkkuṟṟeḻuttu eṉa iruvakaip paṭuttalām eṉpatum peṟappaṭum. uyirmeyk kuṟṟeḻuttu oṉpatum (ka ṅa ca ña ta na pa ma va) uṇmaiyāṉa moḻi mutaṉilaikaḷām eṉpatum taṉi uyirk kuṟṟeḻuttuṭaiya oṉpatum (iṭa, iṉa, iya, ara, ila, aḻa, iḷa, aṟa, aṉa) moḻi mutal vārātavaṟṟai neṭuṅ kaṇakku muṟaippaṭi collutaṟkuk kaiyāṇṭa neṟi eṉpatum teṟṟeṉappaṭum. in neṟikku akaram ikaram eṉṟa iru kuṟṟuyirait tavira vēṟu olikaḷaik koḷḷavillai eṉpatum nōkkattakum.
ravviṟ kammuta lāmuk kuṟilum
valviṟ kimmutal iraṇṭum yavviṟku
iyyum moḻimuta lākimuṉ varumē
moḻi mutal vārāta meyyeḻuttukkaḷukkumuṉ mukkuṟil vantu moḻi mutalākum eṉṟa naṉṉūl vitiyaiyum niṉaika.
4. moḻi mutal varum ka ṅa ca ña ta na pa ma va eṉṟa oṉpatu meykaḷil ṅakaram taṉṉaic collum vaḻi moḻi mutal vantatallatu oru collil vantataṉṟu. eṉavē ṅaṉam moḻi mutalām eṉṟa naṉṉūṟ koḷkai oḷavaikku illai. āṉāl ñakara mutaṉilai paṟṟi ellā ilakkaṇa nūlukkum ātticūṭikkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu.
āe
o eṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya 	(tol. 31)
a ā e ovvo ṭāku ñammutal 	(naṉ. 105)
iṅṅaṉam tolkāppiyam ñakaram mūvuyirōṭum, naṉṉūl nāluyirōṭum moḻi mutalāki varum eṉṟu nūṟpittiruppavum, ñayampaṭavurai eṉṟu oru mutaṉ moḻiyē kūṟuvar oḷavaiyār. ñakaramāvatu akarattōṭu kūṭi varum eṉa uṭaṉpaṭṭa ivar yakaram etaṟkum moḻi mutalākātu eṉṟu karutukiṉṟār. āvoṭu kūṭivarum eṉat tolkāppiyamum a ā u ū o oḷa eṉṟa āṟuyirōṭum varum eṉa vīracōḻiyamum naṉṉūlum kūṟiyiruppa, ātticūṭi yakarattukku oru mutaṉilaiyum koṭātatu viyappāka uḷḷatu. ‘iyalpalātaṉa ceyēl’ eṉṟu moḻi mutalākā eḻuttukkaḷil oṉṟākak koṇṭu ikaravuyirai iṇaikkiṉṟatu. eṉavē ka ca ta pa ña na ma va eṉṟa eṭṭu meykaḷē uṇmaiyāka moḻi mutalāka varum eṉpatu oḷavaiyiṉ eḻuttiyal.
5. muṉṉark kūṟiyapaṭi uyireḻuttu varicaiyum āytanaṭuvum vaittapiṉ patiṉeṭṭu meykaḷukku oru varicai vaippatē oḷavaiyiṉ nōkkam. itu vēṟoru vakaiyālum teḷivupaṭum.
kaṇṭoṉṟu collēl 		... 	14
kaṭivatu maṟa 		... 	32
caṉi nīrāṭu 		... 	16
cakkara neṟinil 		... 	43
tantai tāyp pēṇ		... 	20
takkō ṉeṉattiri 		... 	54
naṉṟi maṟavēl 		... 	21
naṉmai kaṭaippiṭi 		... 	65
paruvattē payircey 		... 	22
paḻippaṉa pakarēl 		... 	76
maṉṟu paṟittuṇṇēl 		... 	23
maṉantaṭumāṟēl 		... 	87
vañcakam pēcēl 		... 	27
vallamai pēcēl 		... 	98
iṅṅaṉam kakaram mutal vakaram varai ovvoṉṟiṟkum iraṇṭu ātticūṭikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mutalāvatāka iruppavai taṉi meyvaricaikkeṉa eḻutappaṭṭavai. iraṇṭāvatāka iruppavai ka kā ki kī eṉa varum uyir mey varicaikku eḻutappaṭṭavai.
6. ātticūṭiyiṉ mutaleḻuttu amaippu evvaḷavu cikkalāṉatu eṉṟu itukāṟum ceyta pākupāṭṭiliruntu ōraḷavu terintatu. itē oḷavai pāṭiya koṉṟai vēntaṉ neṭuṅkaṇakku varicaiyiṟ pāṭappaṭṭatu eṉṟālum taṉimeyvaricaip patiṉeṭṭu eḻuttukkaḷaiyum mēṟkoḷḷa villai. uyirvaricai āytanaṭu uyirmeyvaricai eṉa mūṉṟē aṅku uḷḷaṉa. ātticūṭi nūṟṟeṭṭāka, koṉṟaivēntaṉ 91 ākak kuṟaintataṟkuk kāraṇamtaṉimey varicai iṉmaiyē. pāratiyār putiya ātticūṭiyilum pāratitācaṉ mutalātti cūṭiyilum āytattaiyum taṉimey varicaiyaiyum koḷḷa villai. iḷaiyār ātticūṭiyil pāratitācaṉ āytattaik koḷḷāviṭṭālum, 
kaṇakkil tērcci koḷ
cariyāy eḻutu
tamiḻ uṉ tāymoḻi
nallavaṉāy iru
palliṉait tūymai cey
maṟṟavarkku utavicey
vaṇṭi pārttu naṭa
eṉṟu eḻu taṉimey varicai pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivarum oḷavaiyārpōla uyirmeykkuṟilaip piṉpaṟṟiyiruppatu niṉaiyattakum.
7. uyir āytam mey eṉṟa varicaiyāyvukkuppiṉ, iṉi kakara uyirmey mutalāṉa iṉavaricaikaḷai ārāyvōm. oḷavaiyār karuttupapaṭi, moḻikku mutalil oṉpatu meykaḷ varum eṉiṉum ṅakaram taṉṉai eṭuttu moḻiyum aḷavil varuvatāl ēṉaiyavai eṭṭē.. eṭṭiṉuḷḷum ñakaram ñayampaṭavurai eṉṟa aḷavil aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. atu ña ñā ñi pōṉṟa iṉa varicaikku uriyatillai eṉpatu oḷavaiyār karuttup pōlum. ñaṇṭu-naṇṭu; ñāyiṟu-nāyiṟu; ñāti-nāti eṉṟu iṭaikkālattup palkivanta vaḻakkai nōkki ñakaraviṉattai matiyātu viṭṭārō oḷavaiyār? yakarattai mutaṉ moḻiyākak koḷḷāmaikkum ituvē kāraṇamāka irukkalām. yāṭu-āṭu, yāṉai-āṉai, yāṉi-āḷi, yuttam-uyuttam eṉa yakaram taṉnilai iḻantu varutal kāṇka. tolkāppiyam yā oṉṟu moḻi mutal varum eṉakkūṟavum, vīracōḻiyam naṉṉūl ya yā yu yū yō yau eṉṟu āṟu moḻi mutalām eṉa virintiruppavum yakaram etuvum varātu eṉa avviṉattaiyē oḷavaiyār aṭiyōṭu oḻittirukkiṟār eṉpataṉai moḻivaralāṟṟāḷar uṇara vēṇṭum.
8. ṅakaram ñakaram yakaram immūṉṟiṉ tīyūḻ ātti cūṭiyil ivvāṟāka, ēṉai uyirmey varicaikaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku ottaṉavāka illai. ka ta na pa ma va eṉṉum āṟu meyyum oḷakārattōṭu mutalākum eṉpar tolkāppiyar. ivaṟṟoṭu ca ya eṉṉum iraṇṭaiyum kūṭṭi eṭṭumeyyoṭu oḷa mutalām eṉpar puttamittiraṉārum pavaṇantiyārum. oḷa eṉṟa uyirait taṉ peyarkku mutalākap peṟṟa nam oḷavai yārō eṉiṉ oḷaviyam pēcēl eṉṟu oḷakāram taṉiyuyirāka moḻi mutalāvataṉṟi enta meyyōṭum cērntu vārātu eṉṟu ilakkiyam ceytuviṭṭār. yakarattiṟkuc ceyta puraṭci pōṉṟatu itu. kavvai, tavvai, mavval, pavvam, navvi, cavukkiyam, vavvāl eṉṟu eḻutalām eṉpatu ivar karuttup pōlum. ceyyuḷ vaḻakkaip piṉpaṟṟāmal makkaḷ vaḻakkiṟ kūṟum pēccoliyai muḻutum taḻuvik koṇṭārō oḷavaiyār eṉṟu karuta vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. mutaleḻuttil ivvaḷavu oḻivu ceytavar ātticūṭipōla īṟṟeḻuttukkum oru cūṭipāṭiyiruntāl, avar kālattiṉ oli vaḻakkiṉai evvaḷavō terintukoḷḷa muṭiyum. moḻi mutalāka uyirmey oḷakārattaik koḷḷāmaiyiṉāl kakaramutal makaram varai iṉavaricai 11 āyiṉa. vakaramum muṉṉark kūṟiyapaṭi eṭṭu ākātu ēḻāyiṟṟu. koṉṟaivēntaṉil orē oru vēṟupāṭu. ‘kauvai colliṉ evvarukkum pakai’ eṉṟa ceyyuḷil kakaram oḷakārattōṭu mutalāki vantirukkiṉṟatu.
ātticūṭi 108
koṉṟaivēntaṉ 108 - taṉimey 18=90 + kau = 91
9. moḻi mutal eḻuttukkaḷil palavaṟṟaik kuṟaitta oḷavaiyār ātticūṭiyiṉ iṟutiyil vakaraviṉattai vaikkumpōtu,
	vallamai pēcēl
	vātumuṟ kūṟēl
	vittai virumpu
	vīṭu peṟanil
	uttamaṉāyiru
	ūruṭaṉ kūṭi vāḻ
	veṭṭeṉap pēcēl
	vēṇṭi viṉaiceyēl
	vaikaṟait tuyileḻu
	oṉṉārait tēṟēl
	ōrañ collēl
eṉṟu 11 ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟimuṭippar. ivaṟṟuḷ va vā vi vī ve vē vai eṉṟa ēḻumē vakaraviṉavaricai mutalkaḷ. uttamaṉ, ūruṭaṉ, oṉṉār, ōram eṉṟa nāṉkum uyireḻuttukkaḷ. ivveḻuttukkaḷukku uṭaiyatu viḷampēl, ūkkamatu kaiviṭēl, oppuravoḻuku, ōtuvatoḻiyēl eṉṟu muṉpē cūṭikaḷ taṉiyuyir varicaiyil uḷa. mīṇṭum ivaṟṟukkuc cūṭipāṭuvatu evvakaiyiṟ poruntum? kakaram mutal vakaram varai ellām 11 eṉṟa ōr oḻuṅku vēṇṭi ivvāṟu valintu ceytiruppārā? koṉṟaivēntaṉilum ivvoḻuṅku kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ vaḻakkil ukara ūkāra okara ōkāraṅkaṭku varakavoli iṇaittup pēcuvatu uṇṭu. in nāṉkum itaḻ kuviyolikaḷ; vakaramō pallitaḻ iyaiyap piṟappatu. itaḻiyaipukkum itaḻ kuvivukkum nerukkam uṇṭu. ātaliṉ makkaḷ vāyolikku matippuk koṭutta oḷavaip perumāṭṭi ātticūṭiyai ēṭṭil eḻutiya kālai innāṉku cūṭikaḷaiyum,
	vuttamaṉāyiru
	vūruṭaṉ kūṭivāḻ
	voṉṉārait tēṟēl
	vōrañ collēl
eṉṟu eḻutiyiruppārō? piṉṉar itu iḻivaḻakku, moḻikku aḻivaḻakku eṉṟu eṇṇi eḻutuvōrāl viṭappaṭṭatu pōlum. 108 eṉṟa oru meypporuḷ eṇṇikkaikkāka ivvāṟu cērttiruppār eṉṟu karuta iṭamillai. avvāṟu eṇṇiyirupparēl ñakaraviṉattaiyō yakaraviṉattaiyō taḻuviyiruttal eḷitākavum muṟaiyākavum irukkum. koṉṟaivēntaṉ 91 āka iruttaliṉ oḷavai 108 nampikkaiyuṭaiyavar illai eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.


13. moḻi mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ varampu
tamiḻmoḻikku uriya eḻuttukkaḷ ittuṇaiya eṉavum avai uyirmey, cārpu eṉṟa perumpirivum, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam eṉṟa uṭpirivum paṭum eṉavum ellā ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum toṭakkattē kaṟpippataik kāṇkiṉṟōm. ataṉoṭu amaiyātu coṟku mutalāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṟutiyāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ, iṭainilai mayakkāka varum eḻuttukkaḷ eṉavāṅku mutalīṟiṭainilaikaḷ paravalākac cuṭṭappaṭuvataiyum kāṇkiṟōm. ciṟu vakuppukkut tamiḻ payiṟṟum ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅ kaḷilum mutal īṟu iṭainilai paṟṟiya varampukaḷ iṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. ayalnāṭṭavarkkuk kaṟpikkum payiṟci pōṭṭikaḷilum im muttaṉmaikaḷ olikaḷiṉ āyvoṭu viḷakkam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
tolkāppiyam mutalāvatu nūṉmarapil iṭainilai mayakkattaik kūṟi iraṇṭāvatu moḻi marapil mutaleḻuttu īṟṟeḻuttukkaḷai virivāka iyampum.  naṉṉūl eḻuttiyalil mutalnilai iṭainilai iṟuti nilai mūṉṟaiyum ivvaricaiyiṟ kūṟum vīracōḻiya mutalāṉa nūlkaḷum im marapaip pōṟṟi moḻikiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl mutalīṟu iṭai eṉṟa  mukkūṟu eḻuttilakkaṇa vitikaṭkellām aṭimayam eṉpatu oruvāṟu uṇarappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ moḻi mutaleḻuttiṉ varalāṟum vaḷarcciyum varampum etir nōkkum paṟṟi ik kaṭṭuraiyil cila neṟikaḷai ārāyvōm.
